Dividend for fiscal year 2020 
 
For the fiscal year ending on 31/12/2020, the Board of Directors proposes distribution of 
dividend from the profit of fiscal year 2020 of amount equal to € 7,961,972.10 an amount 
that corresponds to € 0.29 per share. It is noted that the above dividend per share will 
increase depending on the number of Own Shares owned by the Company, at the Record 
Date. The Company’s Own Shares at the date of preparation of the Financial Statements 
amounted to € 963,770. Based on the above, the dividend amounted to € 0.30 per share. 
 
Remuneration through Distribution of Profit 
 
For the fiscal year that ended on 31/12/2020, the Board of Directors proposes the 
distribution of profit to the personnel from the profit of fiscal year 2020, equal to € 
805,757.73 thous., which is equal to € 742.546.00 thous., as remuneration and € 63.211.73 
as labor contributions. 
 
Extraordinary General Meeting of Shareholders of 02/03/2021 “Cancellation of Own Shares 
and Amendment of the Articles of Association” 
 
On March 2, 2021 at 14:00, the Company performed, via teleconference and with the use 
of the special platform of the Athens Stock Exchange, AXIA e-Shareholders Meeting, the 
Extraordinary General Meeting of Shareholders. The General Meeting of Shareholders 
decided and approved the proposal of the Board of Directors and more specifically the 
cancellation, according to article 49 of L. 4548/2018, of nine hundred, ten thousand, three 
hundred, sixteen (910,316) own shares of the Company, the decrease of the share capital 
of the Company by the amount of five hundred, seventy-three thousand, four hundred 
ninety-nine Euro and eight cents (€ 573,499.08), the corresponding amendment of article 
5 of the Article of Association of the Company on the share capital, in order to imprint the 
above decrease and its codification in a unified text, and authorized the Board of Directors 
of the Company in order to undertake all required actions and communications for the 
completion of the above subjects. The decision of the Greek General Commercial Registry 
is expected to be announced at the date of preparation of the Financial Statements.  
 
Provision of a special license of article 101 of Law 4548/2018 

EUROPEAN RELIANCE GENERAL INSURANCE CO.S.A.                                                      
2020 ANNUAL FINANCIAL REPORT 
 
 
211 
 
 
On 27/01/2021, the Company made an announcement in the Athens Stock Exchange and 
the investment website of the Company, on the decision of the Board of Directors of the 
Company for the issuance of a corporate guarantee by “European Reliance General 
Insurance Co. S.A. to Piraeus Bank in favor of the subsidiary “Alter Ego Facilities 
Management S.A.” for the increase of the already existing lending limit of Alter Ego S.A., 
and the decision for the granting of a special license, with duration six (6) months, for the 
preparation and undersigning of the above transaction of the Company with the Company 
with trade name “Alter Ego Facilities Management S.A.”, which is a related undertaking 
with the Company.   
 
The Board of Directors of the Company took into consideration the report of 25/01/2021 
of Ms. Despoina Marinou, Certified Public Accountant (with SOEL No. 17681) of the auditing 
Company “Pricewaterhousecoopers Co.S.A.”, from which it derives that the above 
transaction is fair and reasonable for the Company and its shareholders, which do not 
consist a related undertaking, including the minority shareholders of the Company, 
according to articles 100 and 101 of L. 4548/18.  
 
The entire Assessment Report of the fair and reasonable of the transaction, based on Article 
101 of L. 4548/2018 was presented in the investment website of the Company. Based on 
L. 4548, the shareholders that represent 1/20 of the share capital may request the 
convention of a General Meeting of Shareholders to decide on the subject of provision of 
license, within ten (10) days after the publication of the present announcement. It is noted 
that there has not been any request by the shareholders.  
 
New Law 4706/2020 “Corporate Governance of Société Anonymes” with effective date 
17/07/2021 
 
The Company, after analyzing the Deviations regarding the compliance of the Company 
with L. 4706/2020 on the Corporate Governance of Listed Companies, has planned in the 
first semester of 2021 all necessary actions for the total compliance of the Company with 
the Law. 
Within this framework, the Company has already performed the following actions: 
• Amendment of the Organization Chart. 
• Preparation of a Policy for the Fit and Proper of the Board Members, which has been 
approved by the Board of Directors and will be proposed for approval to the General 
Meeting of Shareholders. 
• Update of the Operation of Regulation of the Risk Management Committee, in 
order to ensure the independence of the Risk Management, as part of the Internal 
Control System. 
• Update of the Operation of Regulation for the Audit Committee, in order to include 
the requirements of the new law, for the function and supervision of the Internal 
Audit Function.

• Update of the Operation of Regulation of the Corporate Governance, Remuneration 
and Nomination Committee, that in European Reliance acts as a Committee, in

For its full compliance with the Law, the Company has scheduled up to 17/07/2021, the 
following actions: 
 
• Election of a new Board Member, taking into consideration the independence 
criteria and diversity, defined by the new Law. 
• Election of a new BoD Committees, taking into consideration the independence 
criteria of their members, as defined by the new Law. 
• Update of the Internal Regulation of Corporate Governance and Operation of the 
Company. 
• Preparation of the Regulation of Operation for the most important subsidiaries. 
• Publication of the documents, as provided by the Law in the Company’s website.

been, or will be disclosed or that will differentiate the funds of the Financial Statements.

At this point, we would like to highlight that the overall pollutants after all transportations 
of the personnel increased, comparing to the previous year, due to the energy consumption 
research that the Company conducted and the number of participants doubled. Moreover, 
due to the Covid-19 pandemic, and the measures for its treatment, the implementation of 
carpooling was limited.  
 
For this reason, the overall pollutants of the Company present slightly increase by 16.8 tn 
CO2, comparing to the respective previous period, a slightly increased equal to 1.0%, mainly 
due to the increase of the pollutants from the transfer of the corporate vehicles, (it refers 
to the new vehicles of the sales contest, that were given in fiscal year 2020 and are over 
30). However, the pollutants from the consumption of the electrical power decreased by 
9.8% and the pollutants from the use of the natural gas are at the same levels with the 
previous year, as a result of over 90% of telecommuting of the personnel.  
 
Furthermore, the Management of the Group promotes the environmental awareness and 
rewards the employees that decide to behave with sensitivity towards the environment in 
their transportations to and from work, and at the same time, the personnel of the 
company promotes this idea.

Recycling 
 
Since 2012, our Group began a recycling program in our central offices and the revenues 
from the collection and sales of the recycling materials, such as paper, plastic and 
aluminum, which shall be donated to vulnerable social groups (since 2013 the revenues 
have been donated to the Institution “Hope” and since 2016 to various community actions 
of Green Angels). 
 
The importance of recycling for the employees of European Reliance can be summarized 
as follows: 
• Reduce of waste and waste management problems 
• Energy and natural resources conservation. Long-term reduction or stability of the 
products’ prices, since raw materials’ production is not required 
• Reduction of air, ground and groundwater pollution 
• Save the energy required for the above products and procedures. 
• Creation of new working positions in energy sustainability fields. 
• Through the recycling of 1 tone of paper, we can save 17 trees and 40,000 liters of 
water, minimize air pollution by 95% and save 130-170 lt of oil. 
European Reliance since the first effort for total waste management in 2012 has 
managed to monthly recycle about 767 kg of various materials (paper, aluminum, 
plastic). More specifically, in 2020 our Group recycled 9.2 tons and destroyed 
confidential documents of weighting 0.28 tons.

The large decrease in recycling of materials (paper, aluminum, plastic) comparing to 2019, 
is mainly due to the increase of the telecommuting of the personnel, with decrease of 
printings by 31%, comparing to the previous period and paper recycling. The above 
decrease in the recycling measures at the same time the limited use of the single-use 
materials (such as single-use plastic glasses, plastic bags, plastic forks and knifes, etc.), as 
well as the environmental awareness of the employees complies with the practices of the 
Group for environmentally friendly materials. The Group also distributed an 
environmentally friendly food bag, environmentally friendly thermos to the personnel. In 
2020, the Group did not perform recycling of lamps, since almost all of them have been 
replaced by led lamps, within the framework of the energy upgrade of the building facilities 
of the Group.

that derive from forests (like wood and paper).

process.
